# Review log: Four spec sites disagree on the finalize workspace-failure envelope

## Standing context

### Settled

- **Direction of the fix**: the §15.1 catalog and the gateway agree, so §6.2 and §7.2 are corrected to them. No workspace envelope is added at finalize (initial draft, 2026-09-25).
- **Session-mode finalize outcome**: `503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED` with `Retry-After` for a staging or `FinalizeWorkspace` failure, and `413 UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` for an `*upload.ValidationError` (handleFinalize in `pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go`; writePodClaimError in `pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go`).
- **Concurrent-workspace finalize**: prepareAtFinalize returns `(nil, nil)` when `MaxConcurrentSessions > 1`, so no workspace failure can surface at `/finalize` on that path.

### Traps

- The archive-limit outcome on the concurrent-workspace `/start` path was not traced. An `*upload.ValidationError` inside a `SlotBindError` classifies as transient and reaches writePodClaimError unwrapped, but a `SlotFailedError` is matched before the archive case. The staged text therefore defers the slot path to §5.2 and names no code for it.
- `SLOT_FAILED` has no §15.1 catalog row; §5.2's **Client error on exhaustion:** bullet describes the envelope's fields only. Do not name `SLOT_FAILED` in staged spec text without adding the row.
- Proposal 0082 appends to the end of the §15.1 finalize row's Notes cell. An edit to that cell here needs a different anchor or coordination with 0082.

### Open

- OD-1, OD-2, and OD-3 in the summary.

### Deferred

## Ledger

### [spec.1.fix-G1.1]
DECISION: SPEC-1 (§6.2 **Client visibility:** bullet) is the one home for the workspace-materialization outcome; SPEC-2 is a pointer to it — BECAUSE §7.2 already defers pre-attached handling to §6.2 by link, and two normative copies had already drifted — ALTERNATIVES: §15.1 finalize row as home (rule spans three endpoints; 0082 appends to that cell; OD-1 is a human call); §7.2 as home (citation loop with §6.2's existing pointer direction).
DECISION: SPEC-1 states an endpoint-generic cascade (archive validator -> 413; concurrent-workspace §5.2 non-retryable category -> §5.2 **Client error on exhaustion:** envelope; else the endpoint's retryable fallback SESSION_CREATION_FAILED / STARTING_FAILED at /{id}/start) — BECAUSE the reserved-slot bind runs no §5.2 retry, so pointing at the §5.2 retry policy misstated the envelope — ALTERNATIVES: name only the endpoint (leaves a §5.2 reader expecting a retry); name SLOT_FAILED (no catalog row).
FACT: Both the combined POST /v1/sessions/start and the two-step /{id}/start bind a concurrent-workspace slot reserved at create through bindConcurrentSlot -> BindReservedSlot -> failReservedSlot -> classifySlotBindFailure; only a non-retryable SlotBindError.Reason becomes SlotFailedError, everything else passes through to writePodClaimError. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go bindConcurrentSlot, failReservedSlot, classifySlotBindFailure; startOnPod `if match.MaxConcurrentSessions > 1`
FACT: *upload.ValidationError carries no GRPCStatus, so on the slot path slotErrCode returns Unknown, the reason is transient, and writePodClaimError's archiveLimit case answers 413. — EVIDENCE: pkg/upload/upload.go ValidationError; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotfailure.go slotErrCode, SlotBindError.Reason
WATCHOUT: SlotBindError.Reason makes PermissionDenied at the workspace_prep stage transient too (it shares the FailedPrecondition case), so a workspace-stage policy rejection is the retryable fallback rather than the §5.2 envelope. The staged text says "a failure in one of the §5.2 categories", which the classifier decides; do not "fix" the spec to enumerate gRPC codes. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotfailure.go SlotBindError.Reason
WATCHOUT: the non-reserved bindSlotWithRetry path (row.PodAssignment empty or recovery) returns SlotFailedError on retry exhaustion even for a transient reason; SPEC-1's cascade describes the reserved-slot start paths only. Resume is outside SPEC-1's scope. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go bindSlotWithRetry `if reason.NonRetryable() || attempt == maxSlotRetries`
MISTAKE: the initial draft deferred the concurrent /start envelope to the §5.2 slot retry policy and narrowed the 413 predicate to "§13.4 extraction ceiling"; both contradicted the catalog row (validators of §13.4 / §7.4, incl. path_escapes_root) and the gateway, and cost a four-finding round.
USEFUL [Traps, first bullet]: the note that an *upload.ValidationError on the slot path reaches writePodClaimError unwrapped saved tracing the 413 branch of the cascade.
CORRECTS [Traps, first bullet]: the archive-limit outcome on the concurrent /start path is now traced: it answers 413 (see FACT above), and the staged text names it; the "therefore defers to §5.2" conclusion no longer holds.
DEFERRED [proposals/0083_fix_four-spec-sites-disagree-on-the-finalize-workspace-failure-envelope/0083_fix_four-spec-sites-disagree-on-the-finalize-workspace-failure-envelope.implementation-checklist.md]: S1 says SPEC-1 and SPEC-2 are "bundled because both restate one outcome"; after this fix SPEC-2 cites the §6.2 bullet SPEC-1 writes, so the bundling reason is that SPEC-2 points at text SPEC-1 lands (SPEC-1 must land first or together).
OPEN: problem-statement.md "What a client receives today" still says the 413 exception is "an upload archive that violates a §13.4 extraction ceiling" and omits the combined POST /v1/sessions/start path and the 413 on the concurrent /start path; the design marked these separate-finding, so a later round corrects them to the §13.4 / §7.4 validator wording.
OPEN: §5.2's slot retry policy says a failed slot is retried on a new slot, yet the reserved-slot bind at start runs no retry; SPEC-1 now states what the gateway does. Whether §5.2 or the gateway is wrong predates 0083 and is shared with the setup-command clause.

### [spec.1.fix-G2.1]
DECISION: SPEC-3 and SPEC-4 delete the `INTERNAL_ERROR` that §16.5 `MinIOUnavailable` and §17.7 **MinIO failure** step (3) name for a failed finalize, and write no replacement code — BECAUSE §6.2 (SPEC-1) is the single home of the finalize outcome and the surrounding text stays true without a code — ALTERNATIVES: replacing with `SESSION_CREATION_FAILED` (third and fourth copies of the §6.2 rule; also misleads, since in a sustained outage the upload step fails first); pointing at §6.2 (higher rung than deletion).

### [spec.1.fix-design-G1.1]

DECISION: SPEC-1 (§6.2 **Client visibility:** bullet) is the one home for the workspace-materialization outcome, stated endpoint-generically: endpoint = `POST /v1/sessions/start` (combined) / `/{id}/finalize` / `/{id}/start` for a concurrent-workspace pool; envelope cascade = archive-validator failure -> 413 UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED; concurrent-workspace slot failure in a §5.2 **Non-retryable failure categories:** reason -> §5.2 **Client error on exhaustion:** envelope; anything else -> the endpoint's retryable fallback (SESSION_CREATION_FAILED, or STARTING_FAILED at /{id}/start). SPEC-2 becomes a pointer to that bullet. — BECAUSE one rewrite closes all four G1 findings, and the cascade mirrors the adjacent setup sentence of the same bullet and the §15.1 SETUP_COMMAND_FAILED row. — ALTERNATIVES: keep deferring /start to §5.2 (false: code runs no retry on a reserved slot and answers 503 STARTING_FAILED or 413); name only the endpoint (leaves the reader following §5.2 to the wrong expectation); home in the §15.1 finalize row (OD-1; rule spans three endpoints and 0082 edits that cell).
FACT: a reserved-slot bind (two-step /{id}/start AND the combined POST /v1/sessions/start on a concurrent pool) goes bindConcurrentSlot -> BindReservedSlot -> failReservedSlot -> classifySlotBindFailure, no §5.2 retry. Only InvalidArgument/ResourceExhausted/PermissionDenied(+FailedPrecondition outside workspace_prep) become SlotFailedError (422); *upload.ValidationError carries no gRPC code, stays transient, reaches writePodClaimError's archiveLimit case (413); everything else is the fallback 503. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go bindConcurrentSlot, failReservedSlot, classifySlotBindFailure, writePodClaimError; start.go startOnPod `if match.MaxConcurrentSessions > 1`; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotfailure.go SlotBindError.Reason
FACT: the §15.1 UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED trigger is "a platform-normative validator defined in §13.4 / §7.4" (includes path_escapes_root, non_regular_entry, symlink); "extraction ceiling" is too narrow. — EVIDENCE: spec/15_external-api-surface.md UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED catalog row
CORRECTS [Standing context / Traps, first bullet]: the concurrent /start archive outcome is now traced: an *upload.ValidationError is answered 413 (not caught by the SlotFailedError case, since classifySlotBindFailure only wraps non-retryable reasons). The staged text no longer defers the slot path to §5.2.
WATCHOUT: do not name SLOT_FAILED in staged text (no §15.1 row); cite the §5.2 **Client error on exhaustion:** bullet label instead. — EVIDENCE: spec/05_runtime-registry-and-pool-model.md **Client error on exhaustion:**
OPEN: §5.2 **Slot retry policy** says a transient slot failure is retried once on a new slot and then answered error.retryable:false; the reserved-slot bind retries nothing and answers the retryable fallback. The same tension already sits in the unedited §6.2 setup sentence and the §15.1 SETUP_COMMAND_FAILED row ("stays the retryable ... STARTING_FAILED fallback"). Pre-existing; a later round or a separate proposal decides whether the reserved-slot seam is exempt from §5.2 retry. Not staged here.
DEFERRED [0083_...implementation-checklist.md]: S1 says SPEC-1 and SPEC-2 are "bundled because both restate one outcome". After G1, SPEC-2 cites SPEC-1; true reason: they land together because SPEC-2 points at the text SPEC-1 writes.

### [spec.1.review-fresh.1]
FACT: The concurrent-workspace archive-limit outcome at `/start` (the review-log Trap "not traced") traces as follows. The two-step `/start` on a slot reserved at create runs BindReservedSlot with no slot retry ("The reserved slot is not re-reserved and retried"). An `*upload.ValidationError` carries no gRPC status, so SlotBindError.Reason() returns transient, classifySlotBindFailure passes it through unwrapped, and writePodClaimError answers 413 UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED. Any other transient materialization failure answers 503 STARTING_FAILED with Retry-After. Only InvalidArgument, ResourceExhausted, or PermissionDenied become SlotFailedError, which answers 422 SLOT_FAILED. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:2631, :2799-2822, :213, :1182; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotfailure.go:97-115; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotbinder.go:347-351
WATCHOUT: "under the §5.2 slot retry policy" in SPEC-1 and SPEC-2 therefore misstates the `/start` envelope. It also conflicts with §13.4's unconditional "Validator violations surface to clients as UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED". Filed. — EVIDENCE: spec/13_security-model.md:750
USEFUL [spec.1.review-citations.1]: the §17.7 MinIO-runbook INTERNAL_ERROR site at spec/17_deployment-topology.md:817 checks out; the blob fetch at finalize is a plain wrap that answers 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED (binder.go:1385-1387, start.go:218-224). Filed independently.
WATCHOUT: SPEC-2 names only `/finalize` and `/{id}/start` for a workspace failure. The combined `POST /v1/sessions/start` also materializes, through startOnPod, and answers SESSION_CREATION_FAILED or 413 (start.go:819, :854). The paragraph's pod-claim clause has the same omission, which predates this proposal, so I did not file it. — EVIDENCE: spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:210

### [spec.1.review-mechanism.1]

FACT: Both staged anchors still match the current spec verbatim after 0081 landed (§6.2 **Client visibility:** bullet, §7.2 **Pre-attached vs. post-attached failure visibility.**). The §15.1 finalize row names CREDENTIAL_POOL_EXHAUSTED and SETUP_COMMAND_FAILED only. — EVIDENCE: spec/06_warm-pod-model.md:292, spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:210, spec/15_external-api-surface.md:646
FACT: A reserved concurrent slot is never retried. failReservedSlot classifies the first failure; a transient reason returns unwrapped, so two-step /start answers 503 STARTING_FAILED and the combined path answers 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED. §5.2 **Slot retry policy** instead retries on a new slot and then returns a non-retryable structured error. Pointing to "§5.2 determines the client error" therefore does not match the shipped transient outcome. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:2622-2628, start.go:2799-2807, start.go:1182, spec/05_runtime-registry-and-pool-model.md:569-578
FACT: The combined POST /v1/sessions/start materializes on both pool kinds (exclusive via prepareAndLaunch, concurrent via BindReservedSlot), and errors route through writePodClaimError with SESSION_CREATION_FAILED. Resume also materializes, through startOnPod with the RESUME_FAILED fallback. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:843-855, start.go:2369-2371, start.go:4067
FACT: writePodClaimError's *upload.ValidationError case covers every §13.4 validator sub-code, path_escapes_root included, and the §15.1 UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED row names path_escapes_root. §13.4's "ceilings" list does not include path traversal, which has its own bullet. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:266-277, pkg/upload/upload.go:134, spec/15_external-api-surface.md:1124, spec/13_security-model.md:738-752

### [spec.1.review-reliability.1]

FACT: The two-step `POST /v1/sessions/{id}/start` on a concurrent-workspace pool binds the slot reserved at create through BindReservedSlot with NO §5.2 retry; failReservedSlot → classifySlotBindFailure maps only a non-retryable reason (oom, workspace_validation, policy_rejection) to 422 SLOT_FAILED, and any transient reason passes through to the retryable 503 STARTING_FAILED fallback with Retry-After. The §5.2 retry loop (applySlotRetryPolicy) runs only on the slotless/recovery path. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:2613-2620 (bindConcurrentSlot), :2630-2631 ("The reserved slot is not re-reserved and retried"), :2799-2819 (classifySlotBindFailure), :1182 (STARTING_FAILED fallback)
FACT: The archive-limit outcome at concurrent-pool /start (the Trap in Standing context): an *upload.ValidationError carries no gRPC status, so SlotBindError.Reason() is transient, classifySlotBindFailure returns it unwrapped, and writePodClaimError's archiveLimit case answers 413 UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotfailure.go:97-115; pkg/upload/upload.go:147 (Error only, no GRPCStatus); pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:204,213
WATCHOUT: Staged text that says "the §5.2 slot retry policy determines the client error" at /start is false for the reserved-slot path; none of STARTING_FAILED, 413, or the absent retry come from §5.2. Filed as a finding. — EVIDENCE: spec-changes.md:32, :48
UNVERIFIED: /{id}/start leaves the row `ready` after a reserved-slot failure ("so the client can retry", start.go:1177-1181) although BindReservedSlot already released the reservation; whether a retried /start can then bind at all was not traced. Code-lane lead, not a spec-loop finding.

### [spec.2.fix-G1.1]

DECISION: SPEC-1 returns to the finalize scope of the sentence it replaces and names the finalize workspace envelope by reference (§15.1 `SESSION_CREATION_FAILED` fallback; §13.4 validator violations as `UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`); SPEC-2 deletes the §7.2 `WORKSPACE_PLAN_INVALID` clause outright — BECAUSE the endpoint-generic cascade was a closed copy of writePodClaimError's per-endpoint mapping, and every round found the next missing endpoint (combined start in round 1, `/resume` and `RESUME_FAILED` in round 2); the §7.2 lead ("a failure surfaces at the endpoint that runs the failing step") already homes the endpoint claim, and the §15.1 rows home the per-endpoint fallbacks — ALTERNATIVES: add `/resume` + `RESUME_FAILED` (grows the same enumeration); scope to "during session creation" (keeps the §5.2 arm split); keep SPEC-2 as a pointer to §6.2 (would credit §6.2 with an endpoint list it no longer states).
MISTAKE: fix round 1 widened SPEC-1 into an endpoint-by-endpoint cascade (combined start, concurrent `/{id}/start`, "otherwise" finalize, a §5.2 non-retryable/exhaustion arm split, and a fallback-code list). It produced two round-2 findings: `/resume` omitted, and a transient concurrent-slot failure stated as the retryable fallback where §5.2 **Max retries:**/**Client error on exhaustion:** retry and then answer retryable:false. Round 2 removed the cascade.
WATCHOUT: do not re-add any endpoint list, fallback-code list, `SLOT_FAILED`, or §5.2 citation to SPEC-1, and do not rewrite SPEC-2 as "surfaces at `POST /v1/sessions/{id}/finalize`" (the finalize-only omission round 1 filed). Concurrent-workspace pools never materialize at finalize (prepareAtFinalize returns nil when MaxConcurrentSessions > 1), so the finalize-scoped SPEC-1 says nothing about slot envelopes. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/finalize.go prepareAtFinalize; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go handleFinalize (`writePodClaimError(w, err, "SESSION_CREATION_FAILED", "workspace finalization failed")`)

### [spec.2.fix-design-G1.1]
DECISION: SPEC-1 returns to the finalize scope of the original sentence and states the finalize workspace envelope by reference (`SESSION_CREATION_FAILED` per the §15.1 catalog, and `UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` via §13.4's own "Validator violations surface to clients as ..." bullet); the endpoint-generic cascade (combined start, concurrent /{id}/start, §5.2 arms, fallback-code list) is DELETED. Adding /resume and RESUME_FAILED, or a §5.2 exemption, is the same enumeration one step larger. — ALTERNATIVES: add /resume + RESUME_FAILED (reviewer option 2: next missing endpoint or code is next round's finding); scope "during session creation" (reviewer option 1: still keeps the §5.2 arm split behind finding 1); stage a §5.2 **Max retries:** exemption for reserved slots (it settles a pre-existing gateway-vs-§5.2 question that 0083 no longer touches, and it belongs in a separate proposal); make SPEC-2 a pointer to a finalize-only §6.2 (the pointer would then claim more than its target states).

A per-endpoint spec list of these therefore has to track every writePodClaimError caller. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go:3144; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:3589, :4058-4067

### [spec.2.review-client-surface.1]

FACT: On the concurrent-workspace materializing paths (two-step /start via BindReservedSlot, combined /v1/sessions/start via ClaimSlot then BindReservedSlot) there is no slot retry: failReservedSlot/classifySlotBindFailure turn only oom/workspace_validation/policy_rejection into SlotFailedError (422 SLOT_FAILED); transient reasons pass through to the endpoint fallback. ### [spec.2.review-reliability.1]
DECISION: filed SPEC-1's concurrent-pool fallback arm against §5.2 **Slot retry policy** — BECAUSE SPEC-1 cites §5.2 **Client error on exhaustion:** for its non-retryable arm only, while that bullet also covers "the retry budget is exhausted" and **Max retries:** (slotRetries default 1, 0 disables) leaves no case where a transient slot failure answers a retryable envelope; the reserved-slot bind has no retry only in code (failReservedSlot) — ALTERNATIVES: leaving it as the pre-existing OPEN (rejected: SPEC-1 is new text that states the conflicting rule, written by fix round 1)
FACT: after a transient STARTING_FAILED on a concurrent-pool reserved slot, BindReservedSlot has already released the reservation (counter DECR, no per-slot key), yet handleStart leaves the row `ready` with PodAssignment set, so a client retry of /start calls BindReservedSlot again on an uncounted slot (and, if the counter hit zero, a claim that ReleaseSlot may have deleted/recycled). — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotbinder.go:212-236, :727-737; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:1175-1183, :2612-2620
UNVERIFIED: whether that retried /start over-subscribes the pod or binds a returned-to-pool pod; code-lane / fidelity reviewer should trace SlotClaimer.ReleaseSlot on the zero edge. Code remedy, outside the spec loop.
FACT: session-mode finalize runs no §6.2 pre-attached retry (failSession then writePodClaimError on the first prepare failure), while §6.2 **Pre-attached failure retry policy:** retries pre-attached failures twice on fresh pods. Pre-existing code-vs-spec gap; SPEC-1's wording ("error on exhaustion" framing) stays consistent with §6.2. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go:3127-3146; spec/06_warm-pod-model.md:285-291
USEFUL [review-log line 52 FACT]: the reserved-slot trace (bindConcurrentSlot -> BindReservedSlot -> failReservedSlot -> classifySlotBindFailure) saved re-deriving the /start envelope.

FACT: All four staged anchors still match the current spec verbatim: §6.2 **Client visibility:** finalize sentence, the §7.2 clause "a workspace-materialization failure surfaces as `WORKSPACE_PLAN_INVALID` at `POST /v1/sessions/{id}/finalize`;", the §16.5 `MinIOUnavailable` parenthetical, and §17.7 "Session creation returns `INTERNAL_ERROR`." — EVIDENCE: spec/06_warm-pod-model.md:292; spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:210; spec/16_observability.md:424; spec/17_deployment-topology.md:817
USEFUL [spec.3.review-reliability.1]: its FACT that handleFinalize reclaims the pod and fails the row before answering 503 settled the reclaim/leak question without a re-trace (sessionserver.go:3128-3145).

### [spec.4.review-security.1]
FACT: Archive decompression and the §13.4/§7.4 validators run in the gateway (Binder.extractOneArchive / gatedExtract in pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go), and the adapter refuses to decompress uploadArchive sources (pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go:494). The 413 bound is therefore sourced from a trusted component, not a pod self-report. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1344, pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go:494
FACT: The adapter's own containment rejections (materialize/writeSymlink via upload.ValidateSymlinkTarget) cross gRPC as codes.InvalidArgument and reach the client as the retryable 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED; the request is still refused, so no control is bypassed. Retryability of that path is OD-2, not a security finding. — EVIDENCE: pkg/adapter/staging.go:306, pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go:313
DECISION: No security finding in round 4 — BECAUSE SPEC-1..SPEC-4 only restate or delete error-envelope names; no control, alert, audit surface, RBAC, or security bound is removed or relaxed (MinIOUnavailable alert keeps its trigger and severity) — ALTERNATIVES: filing the retryable 503 for an adapter containment violation as a fail-closed regression; rejected because the rejection still fails closed and the retry question is the settled open decision OD-2.

### [spec.4.review-single-source.1]
DECISION: no single-source findings in round 4 — BECAUSE SPEC-1's new finalize sentence composes the finalize-scoped outcome from cited homes (§15.1 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED row "claim, materialize, or setup outside a more specific code"; §13.4 "Validator violations surface to clients as UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED"), and no other spec site states a finalize workspace outcome once SPEC-2 deletes the §7.2 clause and SPEC-3/4 delete the INTERNAL_ERROR mentions — ALTERNATIVES: filing SPEC-1 as a copy of the catalog rows (rejected: each code is one clause with a citation)
FACT: after SPEC-2 the only spec site naming the finalize workspace-failure envelope is the §6.2 **Client visibility:** bullet; §7.1 step 11-13, §7.4 staging paragraph, and the §15.1 finalize precondition row name no workspace code — EVIDENCE: spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:446, spec/15_external-api-surface.md:646
WATCHOUT: the §6.2 **Client visibility:** bullet and the §7.2 failure-visibility paragraph both write out the SETUP_COMMAND_FAILED endpoint list (pre-existing, from 0081); it is not staged by 0083, so it is not a (g) finding for this proposal — EVIDENCE: spec/06_warm-pod-model.md:292, spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:210

### [f1.human-decisions]
DECISION: OD-1 resolved as "leave the §15.1 finalize precondition row unedited". In spec-changes.md "Spec sections deliberately untouched", the finalize-row bullet's last sentence now reads "The `SESSION_CREATION_FAILED` catalog row covers the workspace outcome." in place of the open-decision pointer. In summary.md, OD-1 was removed from "Open decisions for human to make", "(see OD-1)" was dropped from the Decisions bullet, the 0082 Watch-out bullet now ends "This proposal does not edit that cell.", and the 0082 impact row now reads "0083 does not edit the §15.1 finalize row 0082's SPEC-1 appends to." with "Nothing." as its action. No checklist change. BECAUSE the SESSION_CREATION_FAILED catalog row covers "claim, materialize, or setup outside a more specific code" (spec/15_external-api-surface.md:1131), and the preconditions table's Notes cells do not name an endpoint's generic fallback: the /start row (spec/15_external-api-surface.md:647) and the /resume row (:650) omit STARTING_FAILED. After SPEC-1, §6.2 cites the finalize row (spec/15_external-api-surface.md:646) only for the envelopes it names. ALTERNATIVES: add a sentence to the row pointing at the §6.2 bullet. Rejected because it creates a second normative copy of the outcome the [spec.1.fix-G1.1] home decision avoids, and because it collides with 0082's append to the same cell (proposals/0082_fix_two-concurrent-post-v1-sessions-id-finalize-calls-for/0082_fix_two-concurrent-post-v1-sessions-id-finalize-calls-for.spec-changes.md:26).
WATCHOUT: the finalize row also omits UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED. The catalog row (spec/15_external-api-surface.md:1124) and §13.4 carry it, so the row convention is "the row names only the envelopes it names" and not strictly "specific envelopes, never fallbacks".
DECISION: OD-2 left to the human and rewritten in summary.md "Open decisions for human to make" as a self-contained entry with recommendation (separate proposal; the staging already carries it), ground, the case for the other answer, alternatives, cost of deciding otherwise, and confidence moderate. The entry drops the old caveat that §6.2's **Non-retryable failures:** bullet could already require a PERMANENT envelope, because that bullet sits under the **Pre-attached failure retry policy:** and governs the gateway's internal retry loop (spec/06_warm-pod-model.md:285, :290). It replaces "a client retries a request that fails identically" with the 409-on-same-session and fresh-session-recurrence account (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go:3143). In spec-changes.md "Edge cases and accepted failure modes", the sentence pointing at OD-2 was deleted from the first bullet. No checklist change. BECAUSE the SESSION_CREATION_FAILED row is TRANSIENT and covers materialize (spec/15_external-api-surface.md:1131), no PERMANENT catalog code covers an adapter containment rejection (spec/15_external-api-surface.md:1124), and a non-retryable answer needs a new code plus a writePodClaimError branch that the first non-goal excludes.
WATCHOUT: the summary's second non-goal still carries "(see OD-2)"; that is correct while OD-2 is open, and a staged change file must not regain a pointer to it.
DECISION: OD-3 resolved as "stage no test". In non-spec-changes.md "Testing", the OD-3 pointer was replaced: the section now reads "This proposal stages no test. The edge case below records what stays unpinned." In "Edge cases and accepted failure modes", the first bullet is retitled "No handler-level test pins the generic finalize materialization envelope.", now names TestWritePodClaimErrorFallback_spec_7_1_4 as pinning writePodClaimError's 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED fallback with Retry-After, narrows the unpinned claim to handleFinalize's routing of a non-archive staging or FinalizeWorkspace failure to that fallback, and drops the OD-3 sentence. In summary.md, OD-3 was removed from "Open decisions for human to make". No checklist change. BECAUSE .claude/rules/test-coverage.md:3 scopes the test obligation to changes under pkg/, cmd/, sdks/, migrations/, and charts/, and :7 opens "Every code change ships with tests"; this proposal stages no code change. The writer-level fallback is already pinned (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/podclaimerror_internal_test.go:226), and handleFinalize passes SESSION_CREATION_FAILED to it (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go:3144). ALTERNATIVES: a tier-1 handler test injecting a FinalizeWorkspace InvalidArgument. Rejected because it adds a code-lane step to a spec-only proposal and would pin the 503 that OD-2 may change.
WATCHOUT: if the non-spec loop stages the comment-only code change from the reconcile.1 OPEN, it falls under test-coverage.md's pure-refactor escape hatch and does not reopen this test decision.
DECISION: OD-4 resolved as "not this proposal's question". In summary.md, OD-4 was removed from "Open decisions for human to make" and recorded as a bullet under "Defects in the shipped tree that this proposal does not stage", headed "§5.2's slot retry policy disagrees with the gateway's bind of a slot reserved at create." and ending "left to a separate proposal, and no proposal stages either answer." No staged change file carried OD-4, so neither was edited. No checklist change. BECAUSE no SPEC-1 to SPEC-4 text states a slot envelope, and the tree conflict is real: §5.2 retries on a new slot and answers error.retryable:false on exhaustion (spec/05_runtime-registry-and-pool-model.md:569, :571, :578), while bindConcurrentSlot sends a reserved slot to BindReservedSlot and failReservedSlot with no retry (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:2612-2620, :2630-2631), reached from both the combined path (:2371) and the two-step path (:2497). Proposal 0081 adds no §5.2 exemption, so no proposal owns the question.
WATCHOUT: the Standing context trap "The staged text therefore defers the slot path to §5.2" is stale; after round 2 no staged text defers to §5.2, and the Open line still names OD-1 to OD-3 only. The round-boundary pass curates both.
DECISION: OD-6 resolved as "keep the title, the directory name, and the problem statement's four-site wording". The staging already carries this answer (status.md title unchanged, no rename staged), so no staged change file and no problem-statement text was edited; in summary.md, OD-6 was removed from "Open decisions for human to make". No checklist change. BECAUSE the four sites in problem-statement.md:7-10 still disagree as stated, and the two further sites SPEC-3 and SPEC-4 edit add to the count without falsifying it; summary.md:11 ("What changes") and summary.md:26 (Goals) already name §16.5 and §17.7; and implemented proposal 0081 cites 0083 by its current title (proposals/0081_fix_a-failed-session-bind-leaves-an-adapter-slot-registry/0081_fix_a-failed-session-bind-leaves-an-adapter-slot-registry.summary.md:842, status Implemented at its status.md:5), which .claude/rules/spec-driven-development.md forbids editing, so a rename leaves a permanently stale label. ALTERNATIVES: renaming the title and directory, rejected for the stale 0081 reference and the cost of moving every prefixed file; a pointer sentence in the problem statement's Statement, not written because "What changes" already lists the extra sites.
WATCHOUT: the OPEN on the "four spec sites" title in [spec.1.fix-G2.1] and the OD-6 routing FACT in [reconcile.1] are closed by this decision; do not re-raise the title from the six edited sites.

### [non-spec.2.fix-G1.1]
MISTAKE: non-spec.1 wrote into the non-spec Design that the adapter's post-promotion symlink re-validation "cannot fail first" because it re-runs the gateway's upload.ValidateSymlinkTarget check. That is false. The gateway check is lexical and runs per entry at the entry's nominal path, while writeSymlink's os.MkdirAll/os.Symlink follow links written earlier in the same build. It cost a round. EVIDENCE: pkg/upload/upload.go ValidateSymlinkTarget (path.Join/path.Clean, no fs lookup); pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go writeSymlink, resolvePath, and the revalidatePromotedSymlinks call in the materialize path.
DECISION: record the 503-vs-413 divergence as a summary bullet under **Defects in the shipped tree that this proposal does not stage**, and cut the non-spec Design to a pointer to that bullet. The proposal stays spec-only and SPEC-1 is unchanged — BECAUSE the divergence predates this proposal (§13.4 already routes every validator violation to UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED), and its root cause is the gateway's unsound lexical pre-check, which is a §13.4 security fix outside the finalize-envelope scope — ALTERNATIVES: an adapter status detail plus a writePodClaimError 413 mapping (adds a wire detail that becomes dead code once the root cause is fixed; OD-2 Alternatives already rejected that direction); staging the root fix here (out of scope, security-side); an edge-case entry (that section is for accepted failure modes of this proposal's own changes); narrowing SPEC-1's 413 clause (already reverted once, because it contradicts §13.4).
WATCHOUT: do not tell the implementor that "the gateway already returns what SPEC-1 states" without the exception. The `d -> .`, `d/link -> ../y` allowSymlinks archive passes the gateway and fails at the adapter with InvalidArgument, which answers 503. EVIDENCE: pkg/adapter/staging.go (FinalizeWorkspace `materialize workspace: %v` InvalidArgument); handleFinalize in pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go (writePodClaimError fallback SESSION_CREATION_FAILED); spec/13_security-model.md §13.4 "Post-promotion symlink re-validation applies." and "Validator violations surface to clients as `UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`".
USEFUL [non-spec.2.fix-design-G1]: its OPEN on out-of-tree link creation is the likely follow-up that owns the root fix. It stays out of the staged text because it was derived by code reading only.

### [non-spec.2.fix-design-G1.1]
FACT: the adapter's post-promotion symlink re-validation IS reachable on input the gateway accepts. upload.ValidateSymlinkTarget is lexical at the entry's nominal path; the binder emits symlink sources in archive order after all mkdir/uploadFile sources; the adapter's writeSymlink does resolvePath (lexical) then os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(dst)) + os.Symlink, both of which follow links written earlier in the same build. `d -> .` then `d/link -> ../y` passes the gateway, lands at build/link, and fails revalidatePromotedSymlinks -> InvalidArgument -> 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED. — EVIDENCE: pkg/upload/upload.go ValidateSymlinkTarget (path.Join/path.Clean, no fs lookup); pkg/upload/archive/archive.go extractTar + builder.symlink; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go appendExtracted; pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go writeSymlink, resolvePath, MaterializeWithPolicy (revalidatePromotedSymlinks after promoteStaging)
CORRECTS [review-log WATCHOUT "do not re-narrow SPEC-1's 413 predicate ... adapter re-check is defence in depth", FACT "adapter's post-promotion re-check is defence in depth behind an identical gateway pre-check", WATCHOUT "the adapter re-check does not fire on reachable input"]: the two checks run the same function but at different locations (nominal vs symlink-followed), so the adapter check fires on reachable input. The "do not re-narrow SPEC-1" half of that WATCHOUT still stands: SPEC-1 must keep §13.4's predicate.
DECISION: record the 503-vs-413 divergence as one bullet in summary.md "Defects in the shipped tree that this proposal does not stage", and cut the non-spec Design's second sentence to a pointer to it; stage no code change — BECAUSE the divergence predates this proposal (§13.4's "Validator violations surface to clients as UPLOAD_ARCHIVE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" already covers "Post-promotion symlink re-validation applies"), and its root cause is the gateway's lexical symlink pre-check, which is a security fix outside a finalize-envelope proposal — ALTERNATIVES: reviewer's adapter status detail + writePodClaimError 413 mapping (new adapter->gateway wire detail that papers over an unsound pre-check; a root fix makes the re-check unreachable again and makes the mapping dead code); Edge-cases entry in non-spec (every other unstaged gateway divergence lives in the summary list).
OPEN (security, high priority, separate finding): the same gap appears to let an archive create symlinks and directories OUTSIDE the adapter's build tree before any re-validation runs. Chain: `d -> .`, `d/s -> ..`, `d/s/t -> ..`, ... each passes the gateway lexically at its nominal path, but each real link sits one level higher, so a later `d/s/t/<name>` entry is created via os.MkdirAll/os.Symlink under /workspace/slots/{sid}/.., potentially inside another session's slot on a concurrent pool. promotion rollback and RemoveAll(buildDir) do not remove it; revalidatePromotedSymlinks walks only root. Derived by code reading only, not executed. A security lens or a separate proposal should verify with a tier-1 materialize test and decide the fix (gateway rejects an entry whose path descends through an earlier symlink entry, or the adapter creates links without following parents). — EVIDENCE: pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go promotionBuildDir (filepath.Dir(root)/<staging name>), writeSymlink, buildResolvedTree; pkg/upload/archive/archive.go builder.symlink (no symlinked-parent check)
WATCHOUT: do not put the out-of-tree creation claim in staged text of 0083; it is unverified and outside the finalize-envelope scope.

### [non-spec.2.review-client-surface.1]
FACT: upload.ValidateSymlinkTarget is purely lexical (path.Join/path.Clean against workspaceRoot/linkPath); it never consults the filesystem. The adapter's writeSymlink/writeUploadFile use lexical resolvePath then os.MkdirAll/os.Symlink, which follow symlinks already written earlier in the same build. So a link written "through" an earlier symlinked directory lands at a different location than the gateway validated. No spec text defines a finalize-bound plan: §7.1 step 11 is "FinalizeWorkspace()", and the §15.1 catalog row names only POST /v1/sessions and /start. This is a pre-existing spec gap. WATCHOUT: the gateway extracts gitClone sources with AllowSymlinks forced true (binder.go:1536). The adapter's writeSymlink still requires the runtime's allowSymlinks (materialize.go:648). As a result, a gitClone repo that contains a symlink, on a runtime that has not opted in, fails at finalize as InvalidArgument and the client sees 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED. Whether that counts as a §13.4 "Symlinks rejected by default" validator violation, which SPEC-1 would route to 413, is unsettled: the spec never states that gitClone passes through the archive validators. I did not file it. A later proposal on gitClone or symlink policy should take it up. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1536, pkg/adapter/workspace/materialize.go:647-650, spec/13_security-model.md:748

DECISION: no findings under the end-to-end mechanism lens in spec round 1 — BECAUSE all four anchors match the current spec verbatim (spec/06_warm-pod-model.md:292, spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:210, spec/16_observability.md:424, spec/17_deployment-topology.md:817), and each hand-written SPEC-3/SPEC-4 claim traces to code: upload Put failure answers 500 INTERNAL_ERROR and records a breaker failure (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/upload.go:419-426), an open breaker answers 503 SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE (upload.go:168-173), session-mode finalize materialization fetches uploads with Blobs.Get and falls to the 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED default (binder.go:1385, 1491; start.go:226-232; sessionserver.go:3128-3145), and a concurrent-workspace pool materializes at /start (finalize.go:238) — ALTERNATIVES: a rule-(g) finding on SPEC-3 and SPEC-4 both stating the upload-first outcome was rejected because the human's OD-5 answer directs both sites to say it.
FACT: POST /v1/sessions (create) touches no blob store, so under a MinIO outage the first failing call for an uploading session is the upload call — EVIDENCE: grep of s.blobs in pkg/gateway/sessionserver (only upload.go, derive.go, session_subresources.go).
FACT: service mode materializes no workspace (spec/05_runtime-registry-and-pool-model.md:391, finalize.go:238), so SPEC-4's finalize-or-/start enumeration of materialization sites is complete for uploaded workspaces.
WATCHOUT: finalize-time archive and gitClone extraction runs inside the Upload Handler gate (binder.go:1552-1566; §7.4 at spec/07_session-lifecycle.md:463), so an open Upload Handler breaker also fails finalize for a gitClone-only session with the 503 fallback. SPEC-4 claims nothing about sessions without uploads, so this does not contradict it. Do not file it against SPEC-4.

### [spec.1.review-performance.1]
FACT: The staged edits are text-only and add no store write, watch, or reconcile; there is no write-rate or failover surface to quantify for this proposal. EVIDENCE: 0083 spec-changes.md SPEC-1..SPEC-4
FACT: The MinIO-outage envelopes in SPEC-3/SPEC-4 match code: an upload blob Put failure answers 500 INTERNAL_ERROR and feeds the per-replica Upload Handler breaker, and an open breaker answers 503 SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE. Finalize does not consult that breaker. A Blobs.Get failure in stageWorkspace/rewriteExtractedSources is plain-wrapped and reaches writePodClaimError's default, which answers 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED with Retry-After. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/upload.go:167-182,419-426; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1385-1387,1491-1493; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:228-234
FACT: The concurrent-workspace /start materializes through the same stageWorkspace, so a MinIO Get failure also surfaces there. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotbinder.go:347
DEFERRED [docs/runbooks/minio-failure.md]: line 103 says "workspace finalize will return `INTERNAL_ERROR`". After SPEC-4 lands, the upload call fails first (500 INTERNAL_ERROR, then 503 once the breaker opens), and a session whose uploads completed fails at finalize with 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED (or at /start on a concurrent-workspace pool). The non-spec staging says it stages no docs change, so the non-spec loop owns this correction.

### [spec.1.review-reliability.1]
FACT: the Upload Handler breaker also gates finalize-time archive extraction. cmd/lenny-gateway/sessiondeps.go:86 wires `w.podBinder.UploadGate = uploadSubsystem`, and gatedExtract (pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1552-1573) runs each archive decode through `UploadGate.Do`. During a MinIO outage an open breaker can therefore also refuse an archive-bearing session at finalize. That refusal has no typed case in writePodClaimError (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:91), so it reaches the 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED default, which is consistent with SPEC-4. EVIDENCE: binder.go:1563-1564
FACT: SPEC-3/SPEC-4 upload claims hold. A downstream blob Put failure answers 500 INTERNAL_ERROR and feeds the breaker (pkg/gateway/sessionserver/upload.go:419-426). An open breaker answers 503 SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE (upload.go:169-172), and the production gateway wires a real breaker (sessiondeps.go:66-70). SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE has no §15.1 catalog row, so the staged "with 503" wording, which names no code, is the right choice. EVIDENCE: grep of spec/ for SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE returns nothing

### [non-spec.1.review-mechanism.1]

FACT: the MinIO-outage trace SPEC-3/SPEC-4 state holds end to end. /upload and /upload-archive share runUpload; a non-client blob Put failure answers 500 INTERNAL_ERROR and feeds the Upload Handler breaker, and an open breaker answers 503 SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE before any Put. The breaker is wired in production. At finalize, validateFinalizeUploadRefs parses URIs only (no MinIO call); the first MinIO touch is Binder.stageWorkspace/extractOneArchive Blobs.Get inside Prepare, whose generic error hits writePodClaimError's default (503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED + Retry-After). Concurrent-workspace pools skip prepareAtFinalize and fetch at /start through slotbinder stageWorkspace. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/upload.go:168-174, upload.go:418-426, cmd/lenny-gateway/sessionsrv.go:361, pkg/gateway/sessionserver/finalize.go:130-160, finalize.go:238, pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1385, binder.go:1491, slotbinder.go:347, pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:233-239

### [non-spec.3.review-mechanism.1]
DECISION: no findings in non-spec round 3 — BECAUSE the only delta since non-spec-r1-prefix is DOCS-1 (clause deletion at docs/runbooks/minio-failure.md:103), checklist S5, and the summary bullets; the remaining runbook text (line 31 "Workspace uploads fail", Step 2 item 1 "new session creation is degraded") agrees with SPEC-3/SPEC-4, and every SPEC-4 outcome traces in code — ALTERNATIVES: filing the §16.5/§17.7 upload-outcome double statement under (g), rejected because the human's OD-5 answer mandates both sites.
FACT: at finalize on a session-mode pool, the first MinIO touch is Blobs.Get inside Binder.stageWorkspace (uploadFile refs) or extractOneArchive (uploadArchive), both before gatedExtract, so a MinIO outage yields an untyped error that writePodClaimError sends to the default 503 SESSION_CREATION_FAILED with Retry-After; handleFinalize, resolveFinalizePlan, and validateFinalizeUploadRefs make no blob-store call. A breaker-open refusal from UploadGate.Do (MinIO back, breaker still cooling) is also untyped and lands on the same default. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1385,1491,1563; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:226-233; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/sessionserver.go:3128-3146
FACT: on a concurrent-workspace pool, the /start stage failure is wrapped as a SlotBindError with slotFailureWorkspacePrep and classified by classifySlotBindFailure (no retry on a slot reserved at create); SPEC-4 names no code for /start, which is correct because the envelope depends on the reason's NonRetryable classification. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/slotbinder.go:347-351; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:2612-2620,2799-2822
USEFUL [non-spec.1.review-mechanism.1]: its end-to-end MinIO trace held and saved re-deriving the upload-side 500/503 path.

### [non-spec.3.review-performance.1]
FACT: The proposal changes only text, adding no store writes, watches, or hot keys, so the top-tier write-rate math is zero. The only claims this lens can test are the MinIO failure-mode claims. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/upload.go:359 (blob Put), :420-426 (a downstream Put error feeds breakerErr and answers 500 INTERNAL_ERROR), :169-172 (an open breaker answers 503 SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE); cmd/lenny-gateway/sessionsrv.go:361 (UploadSubsystem is wired in production).
FACT: Finalize-time materialization reads uploads back from the blob store (binder.go:1385, :1491 Blobs.Get), so a MinIO outage after the uploads complete fails at finalize, as SPEC-4 states. prepareAtFinalize also returns early for service mode, which materializes no workspace (finalize.go:238). The SPEC-4 enumeration therefore needs no service-mode clause.
WATCHOUT: docs/runbooks/slo-session-creation.md:64 maps INTERNAL_ERROR to minio-failure under a metric (lenny_session_creation_failed_total) that exists nowhere in spec/ or pkg/. This drift predates the proposal and DOCS-1 does not cause it, so it is not a finding for 0083.

### [non-spec.3.review-reliability.1]
DECISION: no reliability findings in non-spec round 3 — BECAUSE the r1-prefix->current delta is DOCS-1 (a clause deletion in docs/runbooks/minio-failure.md Step 2 item 1), checklist S5, and summary lines, none of which adds a retry, lease, reclaim, or recovery mechanism; SPEC-3/SPEC-4's upload-first cascade still matches runUpload (Put failure sets breakerErr and answers 500 INTERNAL_ERROR; an open breaker answers 503 SUBSYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE before any Put) and §4.1's per-replica automatic breaker — ALTERNATIVES: filing an unbounded MinIO wait on the finalize path (rejected: pre-existing and not touched by any staged edit; see FACT below).
FACT: the binder's finalize-time Blobs.Get reaches miniostore.Get, which uses context.Background() and a minio.New client built with default options, so a blackholed MinIO is bounded only by minio-go's default transport timeouts and its internal retry loop, not by a request deadline. SPEC-4's "fails when its workspace is materialized" still holds eventually; the delay is pre-existing and outside 0083. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/binder.go:1385, pkg/blobstore/miniostore/miniostore.go:164-167,566-573
USEFUL [non-spec.1.review-reliability.1]: its FACT that a finalize prepare failure reclaims the pod and fails the row settled the leak question without a re-trace.

### [non-spec.3.review-security.1]

DECISION: no security findings in non-spec round 3 — BECAUSE the only delta since the r1 snapshot is DOCS-1 (deletes the "workspace finalize will return `INTERNAL_ERROR`" clause from docs/runbooks/minio-failure.md Step 2 item 1), checklist S5, and summary lines; the tenant notice ("new session creation is degraded") stays, and the MinIOUnavailable alert's expr, severity, and runbook link are untouched in pkg/alerting/rules/rules.go and its rendered copies — ALTERNATIVES: none worth filing.
FACT: the MinIOUnavailable rule's Description in pkg/alerting/rules/rules.go ("Blocks workspace uploads at session creation and seal-and-export at session termination") names no finalize code, so SPEC-3/SPEC-4 leave no stale INTERNAL_ERROR in the alert catalog or its generated copies (docs/alerting/rules.yaml, charts/lenny/files/alerting-rules.yaml, pkg/embedded/manifests/manifests.yaml). After DOCS-1, the only remaining "finalize ... INTERNAL_ERROR" sites in spec/docs are the two SPEC-3/SPEC-4 anchors themselves. — EVIDENCE: pkg/alerting/rules/rules.go:357-365; grep over docs spec schemas charts pkg
USEFUL [non-spec.2.review-security.1]: its no-control-regression reasoning for DOCS-1 holds unchanged.

### [non-spec.3.review-single-source.1]

DECISION: no (g) finding on SPEC-3 and SPEC-4 both stating "upload fails INTERNAL_ERROR, then 503 once the Upload Handler breaker opens" — BECAUSE the human's OD-5 answer directs that both sites say it, and each states it in one parenthetical/clause citing §4.1 — ALTERNATIVES: reducing §17.7 to a citation of the §16.5 row; rejected as reopening a human decision.
FACT: no other spec site names INTERNAL_ERROR for an upload or finalize under MinIO outage; after SPEC-3/SPEC-4 the only two are those staged texts — EVIDENCE: grep of spec/*.md hits only spec/16_observability.md:424 and spec/17_deployment-topology.md:817
FACT: the MinIOUnavailable alert rule description (pkg/alerting/rules/rules.go:357-364, rendered into docs/alerting/rules.yaml:67 and charts/lenny/files/alerting-rules.yaml:64) and docs/reference/metrics.md:477 name no finalize code, so SPEC-3 leaves them correct; docs/runbooks/minio-unavailable.md is a generic stub with no finalize claim.
FACT: DOCS-1 anchor matches docs/runbooks/minio-failure.md:103 verbatim; nothing else on that page names a finalize or upload code.

### [non-spec.3.review-test-coverage.1]
DECISION: no test-coverage finding in non-spec round 3 — BECAUSE the r1-prefix->current delta is DOCS-1 (a clause deletion in docs/runbooks/minio-failure.md Step 2 item 1), checklist S5 (tiers 0, 11), and summary/index lines; the deletion changes no behavior, no existing tier-11 gate reads the runbook body, and test-coverage.md:3 scopes the test obligation to pkg/, cmd/, sdks/, migrations/, charts/ — ALTERNATIVES: a tier-11 pin modeled on TestTokenServiceOutageRunbookAttributesToFinalize (tests/tier11_docs/session_creation_phases_test.go:83) asserting the finalize INTERNAL_ERROR clause is gone (rejected: regression hardening for a deleted wrong claim; the round-2 mechanism lens rejected the same idea); re-filing the handler-level finalize 503 test (settled in earlier rounds).
CORRECTS [non-spec.1.review-test-coverage.1]: its WATCHOUT that DOCS-1 "should add the analogous tier-11 test in the same step" states a precedent, not a requirement; the precedent test pins a moved attribution (a positive claim), while DOCS-1 only deletes a clause and restates no code.
FACT: TestWritePodClaimErrorFallback_spec_7_1_4 asserts code, message, and Retry-After=5 but not the HTTP 503 status line; the 503 on writePodClaimError's default branch is asserted via handler tests elsewhere (e.g. pkg/gateway/sessionserver/create_test.go:755-788). Not material to the edge-case bullet. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/podclaimerror_internal_test.go:226-252, pkg/gateway/sessionserver/start.go:227-233
